```swift
// Test fixture file: readers of field outputs and a sampling shortcut, shared by the field nodes' tests.
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import Testing

extension EvaluationReport {
    /// The one field on an output, or `nil` when it holds none or several.
    func field(_ node: Node, _ socket: SocketName = "field") -> Field? {
        if case .one(.field(let field))? = value(node, socket) { field } else { nil }
    }

    /// Every field on an output, in order (a list of fields from a broadcast).
    func fields(_ node: Node, _ socket: SocketName = "field") -> [Field] {
        (value(node, socket)?.items ?? []).compactMap { if case .field(let field) = $0 { field } else { nil } }
    }
}

/// `field` sampled at `points` (the field must exist).
func sampled(_ field: Field?, at points: [Vector3], kernel: any Kernel = FakeKernel()) async throws -> [Double] {
    let field = try #require(field, "the node made no field")
    return try await FieldSampler.sample(field, at: points, kernel: kernel)
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/DistanceFieldNodeTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorOCCT
import Testing

/// Distance Field (fields spec §3.1): a point, edges, a profile, faces or a solid; signed for a solid, negative inside.
struct DistanceFieldNodeTests {
    func direct(_ target: Scalar?, mode: Int = 1) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        var slots: [SocketName: NodeInputs.Slot] = ["mode": .item(.integer(mode))]
        if let target { slots["target"] = .item(target) }
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(DistanceFieldNode.typeID)
        return try await DistanceFieldNode.evaluate(NodeInputs(item: 0, slots: slots), kernel: FakeKernel(),
                                                    context: EvalContext(node: node, item: 0, parameters: [:]))
    }

    func message(_ target: Scalar?) async -> String? {
        do {
            _ = try await direct(target)
            return nil
        } catch {
            return Evaluator.message(for: error)
        }
    }

    // MARK: through the evaluator

    @Test func aSolidIsSignedByDefaultAndNegativeInside() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: distance, "target")
        let report = try await h.run([distance])
        #expect(report.field(distance)?.description == "distance(solid)")
        let found = try await sampled(report.field(distance), at: [Vector3(0, 0, 5), Vector3(5, 0, 5), Vector3(8, 0, 5)])
        #expect(found == [-5, 0, 3])
    }

    @Test func unsignedMeasuresToTheSurfaceFromInside() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self, ["mode": .integer(0)])
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: distance, "target")
        let report = try await h.run([distance])
        #expect(report.field(distance)?.description == "distance(solid, unsigned)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(distance), at: [Vector3(0, 0, 5), Vector3(8, 0, 5)]) == [5, 3])
    }

    @Test func aPointIsMeasuredInPlainMaths() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let point = h.add(VectorNode.self, ["x": .number(1), "y": .number(2), "z": .number(3)])
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(point, "vector", to: distance, "target")
        let report = try await h.run([distance])
        #expect(report.field(distance)?.description == "distance(point)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(distance), at: [Vector3(4, 6, 3)]) == [5], "signed is ignored for a point")
    }

    @Test func aProfileIsMeasuredToItsLoops() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let rectangle = h.add(RectangleNode.self, ["width": .number(10), "height": .number(10)])
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(rectangle, "profile", to: distance, "target")
        let report = try await h.run([distance])
        #expect(report.field(distance)?.description == "distance(profile)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(distance), at: [Vector3(0, 0, 0), Vector3(8, 0, 0)]) == [5, 3])
    }

    @Test func anEdgeSetIsMeasuredToItsEdgesAndSignedIsIgnored() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let box = h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let edges = h.add(AllEdgesNode.self)
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)  // Signed, the default
        h.wire(box, "solid", to: edges, "solid")
        h.wire(edges, "edges", to: distance, "target")
        let report = try await h.run([distance])
        #expect(report.isOK(distance))
        #expect(report.field(distance)?.description == "distance(edges)")
        let found = try await sampled(report.field(distance), at: [Vector3(0, 0, 5)])
        #expect(abs(found[0] - 50.0.squareRoot()) < 1e-12, "the cube's centre is 5√2 from its nearest edge")
    }

    // MARK: refusals

    @Test func withoutATargetTheNodeSaysWhatToWire() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        let report = try await h.run([distance])
        #expect(report.error(distance) == "Distance Field needs a target: wire a point, edges, a profile, faces or a solid.")
    }

    @Test func aNumberIsNotATarget() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let number = h.add(NumberNode.self)
        let distance = h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(number, "value", to: distance, "target")
        let report = try await h.run([distance])
        #expect(report.error(distance) == "Distance Field can't measure from a number: wire a point, edges, a profile, faces or a solid.")
    }

    @Test func anOutOfRangeModeIsRefused() async throws {
        #expect(await message(.vector(.zero)) == nil)
        do {
            _ = try await direct(.vector(.zero), mode: 7)
            Issue.record("expected a refusal")
        } catch {
            #expect(Evaluator.message(for: error) == "Choose Unsigned or Signed for “mode”.")
        }
    }

    @Test func emptySelectionsBadPointsAndBlankProfilesAreRefusedInWords() async throws {
        let solid = try await FakeKernel().extrude(.rectangle(width: 10, height: 10, plane: .xy), distance: 10, mode: .oneSided,
                                                   tag: NodeTag(node: NodeID(), item: 0))
        let nothing = "are empty: there is nothing to measure from."
        #expect(await message(.edgeSet(EdgeSet(solid: solid, edges: []))) == "Distance Field's edges \(nothing)")
        #expect(await message(.faceSet(FaceSet(solid: solid, faces: []))) == "Distance Field's faces \(nothing)")
        #expect(await message(.vector(Vector3(.nan, 0, 0))) == "Distance Field's point isn't a finite position.")
        #expect(await message(.profile(Profile2D(plane: .xy, outer: []))) == "Distance Field's profile has no outline.")
        #expect(await message(.edgeSet(EdgeSet(solid: solid, edges: [EdgeID(500)]))) == "A selected edge doesn't exist on the solid.")
        #expect(await message(.faceSet(FaceSet(solid: solid, faces: [FaceID(500)]))) == "A selected face doesn't exist on the solid.")
        #expect(await message(.faceSet(FaceSet(solid: solid, faces: [FaceID(0)]))) == nil)
    }

    // MARK: faces, on OCCT

    @Test func aFaceSetIsMeasuredToThoseFacesOnOCCT() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        let solid = try await kernel.extrude(.rectangle(width: 10, height: 10, plane: .xy), distance: 10, mode: .oneSided,
                                             tag: NodeTag(node: NodeID(), item: 0))
        let top = try #require(solid.topology.faces.first { $0.kind == .plane && ($0.normal?.z ?? 0) > 0.5 })
        let outputs = try await direct(.faceSet(FaceSet(solid: solid, faces: [top.id])))
        guard case .field(let field)? = outputs.values["field"] else {
            Issue.record("no field")
            return
        }
        #expect(field.description == "distance(faces)")
        let found = try await FieldSampler.sample(field, at: [Vector3(1, 2, 3), Vector3(0, 0, 10)], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(abs(found[0] - 7) < 1e-7 && abs(found[1]) < 1e-7)
    }
}
```

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Linear Gradient, Radial Gradient and Noise Field (fields spec §3.1).
struct GradientAndNoiseNodeTests {
    // MARK: Linear Gradient

    @Test func aLinearGradientIsZeroOnThePlaneAndOneALengthAlongTheNormal() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let plane = h.add(PlaneNode.self, ["offset": .number(2)])
        let gradient = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(5)])
        h.wire(plane, "plane", to: gradient, "plane")
        let report = try await h.run([gradient])
        #expect(report.field(gradient)?.description == "linear(length 5)")
        let points = [Vector3(9, 9, 2), Vector3(0, 0, 7), Vector3(0, 0, -3), Vector3(1, 1, 12)]
        let found = try await sampled(report.field(gradient), at: points)
        #expect(found == [0, 1, -1, 2], "unclamped, either side")
    }

    @Test func theDefaultLinearGradientIsTenMillimetresAlongZ() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let gradient = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self)
        let report = try await h.run([gradient])
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(gradient), at: [Vector3(0, 0, 10), Vector3(0, 0, 5)]) == [1, 0.5])
    }

    @Test func aLinearGradientRefusesAnEmptyLengthOrAPlaneWithoutADirection() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let flat = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(0)])
        let negative = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(-2)])
        let blind = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["plane": .plane(Plane(origin: .zero, normal: .zero, xAxis: .unitX))])
        let report = try await h.run([flat, negative, blind])
        #expect(report.error(flat) == "“length” must be greater than 0 mm.")
        #expect(report.error(negative) == "“length” must be greater than 0 mm.")
        #expect(report.error(blind) == "“plane” has no direction.")
    }

    // MARK: Radial Gradient

    @Test func aRadialGradientIsZeroAtTheCentreAndOneAtTheRadius() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let centre = h.add(VectorNode.self, ["x": .number(1), "y": .number(1), "z": .number(1)])
        let gradient = h.add(RadialGradientNode.self, ["radius": .number(2)])
        h.wire(centre, "vector", to: gradient, "center")
        let report = try await h.run([gradient])
        #expect(report.field(gradient)?.description == "radial(radius 2)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(gradient), at: [Vector3(1, 1, 1), Vector3(1, 1, 3), Vector3(1, 1, -3)]) == [0, 1, 2])
    }

    @Test func aRadialGradientRefusesAnEmptyRadius() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let zero = h.add(RadialGradientNode.self, ["radius": .number(0)])
        let report = try await h.run([zero])
        #expect(report.error(zero) == "“radius” must be greater than 0 mm.")
    }

    // MARK: Noise Field

    @Test func theNoiseFieldHasTheSeedsNoise() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let noise = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["seed": .integer(42), "scale": .number(10), "octaves": .integer(1)])
        let report = try await h.run([noise])
        #expect(report.field(noise)?.description == "noise(seed 42, scale 10, octaves 1)")
        let points = [Vector3(1, 2, 3), Vector3(-4.5, 0.25, 7), Vector3(10, 10, 10)]
        let expected = FieldNoise(seed: 42, scale: 10, octaves: 1)
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(noise), at: points) == points.map { expected.value(at: $0) })
    }

    @Test func theSameSeedIsTheSameFieldAndAnotherSeedIsAnother() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let a = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["seed": .integer(5)]), again = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["seed": .integer(5)])
        let other = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["seed": .integer(6)])
        let report = try await h.run([a, again, other])
        let points = (0..<20).map { Vector3(Double($0) * 1.3 + 0.1, Double($0) * 0.7, 2.2) }
        let first = try await sampled(report.field(a), at: points)
        #expect(first == (try await sampled(report.field(again), at: points)))
        #expect(first != (try await sampled(report.field(other), at: points)))
    }

    @Test func theNoiseFieldRefusesAnEmptyScaleAndOctavesOutsideOneToSix() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let flat = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["scale": .number(0)])
        let none = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["octaves": .integer(0)])
        let many = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["octaves": .integer(7)])
        let six = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["octaves": .integer(6)])
        let report = try await h.run([flat, none, many, six])
        #expect(report.error(flat) == "“scale” must be greater than 0 mm.")
        #expect(report.error(none) == "“octaves” must be from 1 to 6.")
        #expect(report.error(many) == "“octaves” must be from 1 to 6.")
        #expect(report.isOK(six))
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Sources/DistanceFieldNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// A field of the distance to something (fields spec §3.1): a point, the edges of an edge set, a profile's loops, faces or a
/// solid, in millimetres. To a solid it is signed (negative inside) unless Unsigned is chosen; to anything else it is unsigned
/// whichever mode is set, so the default Signed never refuses an edge set. The node only builds the field: no kernel call
/// happens until a node samples it (`FieldSampler`), which asks the kernel once per batch and caches the answer.
public enum DistanceFieldNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.distanceField"
    public static let displayName = "Distance Field"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let modes = ["Unsigned", "Signed"]
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("target", .any, optional: true),
        SocketSpec("mode", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(1)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Distance", controls: [.segmented("mode", options: modes)])]

    private static let wiring = "wire a point, edges, a profile, faces or a solid."

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        guard inputs.has("target") else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("Distance Field needs a target: \(wiring)") }
        let signed = try inputs.choice("mode", options: modes) == 1
        let source = try source(of: try inputs.scalar("target"))
        try checkSelection(source)
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.distance(source, signed: signed))])
    }

    /// What the wired value is measured as, or why it can't be.
    private static func source(of target: Scalar) throws -> Field.DistanceSource {
        switch target {
        case .vector(let point):
            guard point.isFinite else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("Distance Field's point isn't a finite position.") }
            return .point(point)
        case .profile(let profile):
            guard !profile.outer.isEmpty else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("Distance Field's profile has no outline.") }
            return .profile(profile)
        case .edgeSet(let set):
            return .kernel(.edges(set))
        case .faceSet(let set):
            return .kernel(.faces(set))
        case .solid(let solid):
            return .kernel(.solid(solid))
        default:
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Distance Field can't measure from \(target.type.indefiniteName): \(wiring)")
        }
    }

    /// An empty selection is this node's error; an edge or face the solid lacks is the kernel's wording.
    private static func checkSelection(_ source: Field.DistanceSource) throws {
        switch source {
        case .kernel(.edges(let set)) where set.edges.isEmpty:
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Distance Field's edges are empty: there is nothing to measure from.")
        case .kernel(.faces(let set)) where set.faces.isEmpty:
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Distance Field's faces are empty: there is nothing to measure from.")
        case .kernel(let target):
            try target.validate()
        case .point, .profile:
            return
        }
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Sources/LinearGradientNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// A field that grows along a plane's normal (fields spec §3.1): 0 on the plane, 1 one `length` along the normal, unclamped,
/// negative behind it.
public enum LinearGradientNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.linearGradient"
    public static let displayName = "Linear Gradient"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("plane", .plane, defaultValue: .plane(.xy)),
        SocketSpec("length", .number, defaultValue: .number(10), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.1...1000),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Gradient", controls: [.planePicker("plane"), .slider("length")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let plane = try inputs.plane("plane")
        let length = try inputs.number("length")
        guard length.isFinite, length > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“length” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        guard plane.origin.isFinite, let normal = plane.normal.normalized else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("“plane” has no direction.")
        }
        let unit = Plane(origin: plane.origin, normal: normal, xAxis: plane.xAxis)
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.linearGradient(plane: unit, length: length))])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Sources/RadialGradientNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// A field of the distance from a centre over a radius (fields spec §3.1): 0 at the centre, 1 at the radius, unclamped.
public enum RadialGradientNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.radialGradient"
    public static let displayName = "Radial Gradient"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("center", .vector, defaultValue: .vector(.zero), unit: .millimetres),
        SocketSpec("radius", .number, defaultValue: .number(10), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.1...1000),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Gradient", controls: [.vector("center"), .slider("radius")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let center = try inputs.vector("center")
        let radius = try inputs.number("radius")
        guard radius.isFinite, radius > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“radius” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        guard center.isFinite else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“center” isn't a finite position.") }
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.radialGradient(center: center, radius: radius))])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Sources/NoiseFieldNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// Smooth fractal noise in 0…1 (fields spec §3.1), seeded: the same seed, scale and octaves give the same field on every
/// machine (`FieldNoise`, hashed with `SeededRandom`). `scale` is the size of the biggest features in millimetres; each of
/// the `octaves` (1 to 6) halves the size and the strength of the one before.
public enum NoiseFieldNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.noiseField"
    public static let displayName = "Noise Field"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("seed", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(0)),
        SocketSpec("scale", .number, defaultValue: .number(10), unit: .millimetres, range: 0.1...100),
        SocketSpec("octaves", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(1), unit: .count, range: 1...6),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Noise", controls: [.integer("seed"), .slider("scale"), .integer("octaves")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let scale = try inputs.number("scale")
        let octaves = try inputs.integer("octaves")
        guard scale.isFinite, scale > 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“scale” must be greater than 0 mm.") }
        guard (1...6).contains(octaves) else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“octaves” must be from 1 to 6.") }
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.noise(seed: try inputs.integer("seed"), scale: scale, octaves: octaves))])
    }
}
```

- Consumes: `Field`, `NodeInputs.field`, `FieldSampler` (Tasks 3, 6), the Task 7 test helpers `report.field(_:_:)`, `report.fields(_:_:)`, `sampled(_:at:kernel:)`.
- Produces: `FieldAddNode` (`creator.fieldAdd`, "Add"), `FieldMultiplyNode` (`creator.fieldMultiply`, "Multiply"),
  `FieldMinMaxNode` (`creator.fieldMinMax`, "Min / Max"), `FieldRemapNode` (`creator.fieldRemap`, "Remap"),
  `FieldClampNode` (`creator.fieldClamp`, "Clamp"), `FieldSmoothBlendNode` (`creator.fieldSmoothBlend`, "Smooth Blend"); each
  outputs `field`.

All six take item sockets, so they broadcast over a list or tree of fields like any node. A `.field` socket accepts a
number or an integer (D4): wired, it arrives as a constant field; typed in the inspector (`b` = 0.5 on Multiply), the stored
number is converted the same way. Defaults are the identity of the operation (0 for Add, 1 for Multiply). Remap's and
Clamp's ranges, and Smooth Blend's width, are plain number sockets (a field can't be a range end).

Messages: "Remap's from range is empty (both ends are 3)." (the spec's example reads "3 mm", but a field has no unit
until it is sampled, so the unit is left out; see the plan's user decisions), "Remap's ranges must be finite numbers.",
"Clamp's low end (3) is above its high end (1).", "Clamp's limits must be finite numbers.", "“width” must be 0 or more.",
"Choose Min or Max for “mode”.".

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Add, Multiply, Min / Max, Remap, Clamp and Smooth Blend (fields spec §3.2), against hand-worked values.
struct FieldMathsNodeTests {
    /// A field of the x coordinate: a Linear Gradient along +X of length 1.
    func xField(_ h: inout Harness) -> Node {
        let plane = h.add(PlaneNode.self, ["orientation": .integer(2)])  // YZ: its normal is +X
        let gradient = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(1)])
        h.wire(plane, "plane", to: gradient, "plane")
        return gradient
    }

    let line = [Vector3(-10, 0, 0), Vector3(0, 0, 0), Vector3(10, 0, 0), Vector3(20, 0, 0), Vector3(30, 0, 0)]

    // MARK: Add and Multiply

    @Test func addAddsTwoFieldsAndTwoTypedNumbersAreConstantFields() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let add = h.add(FieldAddNode.self, ["a": .number(2), "b": .number(3)])
        let report = try await h.run([add])
        #expect(report.field(add)?.description == "2 + 3")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(add), at: [.zero, Vector3(9, 9, 9)]) == [5, 5])
    }

    @Test func multiplyByPointFiveTypedIntoB() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let x = xField(&h)
        let multiply = h.add(FieldMultiplyNode.self, ["b": .number(0.5)])
        h.wire(x, "field", to: multiply, "a")
        let report = try await h.run([multiply])
        #expect(report.field(multiply)?.description == "linear(length 1) × 0.5")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(multiply), at: line) == [-5, 0, 5, 10, 15])
    }

    @Test func theDefaultsAreTheIdentities() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let add = h.add(FieldAddNode.self), multiply = h.add(FieldMultiplyNode.self)
        let report = try await h.run([add, multiply])
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(add), at: [.zero]) == [0])
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(multiply), at: [.zero]) == [1])
    }

    @Test func aNumberOrIntegerNodeWiresStraightIntoAField() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let number = h.add(NumberNode.self, ["value": .number(4.5)]), whole = h.add(IntegerNode.self, ["value": .integer(2)])
        let add = h.add(FieldAddNode.self)
        h.wire(number, "value", to: add, "a")
        h.wire(whole, "value", to: add, "b")
        let report = try await h.run([add])
        #expect(report.field(add)?.description == "4.5 + 2")
    }

    @Test func aListOfNumbersBroadcastsIntoAListOfFields() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let series = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["count": .integer(3)])
        let add = h.add(FieldAddNode.self, ["b": .number(10)])
        h.wire(series, "values", to: add, "a")
        let report = try await h.run([add])
        #expect(report.fields(add).map(\.description) == ["0 + 10", "1 + 10", "2 + 10"])
    }

    @Test func addingAnAdditionInsideAMultiplyIsBracketed() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let add = h.add(FieldAddNode.self, ["a": .number(1), "b": .number(2)])
        let multiply = h.add(FieldMultiplyNode.self, ["b": .number(3)])
        h.wire(add, "field", to: multiply, "a")
        let report = try await h.run([multiply])
        #expect(report.field(multiply)?.description == "(1 + 2) × 3")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(multiply), at: [.zero]) == [9])
    }

    // MARK: Min / Max

    @Test func minAndMaxPickTheLowerOrHigherField() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let x = xField(&h)
        let low = h.add(FieldMinMaxNode.self, ["mode": .integer(0), "b": .number(5)])
        let high = h.add(FieldMinMaxNode.self, ["mode": .integer(1), "b": .number(5)])
        h.wire(x, "field", to: low, "a")
        h.wire(x, "field", to: high, "a")
        let report = try await h.run([low, high])
        #expect(report.field(low)?.description == "min(linear(length 1), 5)")
        #expect(report.field(high)?.description == "max(linear(length 1), 5)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(low), at: line) == [-10, 0, 5, 5, 5])
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(high), at: line) == [5, 5, 10, 20, 30])
    }

    @Test func anOutOfRangeModeIsRefused() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let node = h.add(FieldMinMaxNode.self, ["mode": .integer(4)])
        let report = try await h.run([node])
        #expect(report.error(node) == "Choose Min or Max for “mode”.")
    }

    // MARK: Remap

    @Test func remapMapsAndClampsLikeTheSpecsExample() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let x = xField(&h)
        let remap = h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(0), "fromHigh": .number(20), "toLow": .number(1), "toHigh": .number(0),
        ])
        h.wire(x, "field", to: remap, "field")
        let report = try await h.run([remap])
        #expect(report.field(remap)?.description == "remap(linear(length 1), 0…20 → 1…0)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(remap), at: line) == [1, 1, 0.5, 0, 0])
    }

    @Test func switchingClampOffLetsTheMapRunOn() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let x = xField(&h)
        let remap = h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(0), "fromHigh": .number(20), "toLow": .number(1), "toHigh": .number(0), "clamp": .bool(false),
        ])
        h.wire(x, "field", to: remap, "field")
        let report = try await h.run([remap])
        #expect(report.field(remap)?.description == "remap(linear(length 1), 0…20 → 1…0, unclamped)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(remap), at: line) == [1.5, 1, 0.5, 0, -0.5])
    }

    @Test func clampStartsOn() {
        #expect(FieldRemapNode.inputs.first { $0.name == "clamp" }?.defaultValue == .bool(true))
        #expect(BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(FieldRemapNode.typeID).inputValues["clamp"] == nil, "a socket default, not a stored setting")
    }

    @Test func anEmptyFromRangeIsRefusedWithItsNumber() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let remap = h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, ["fromLow": .number(3), "fromHigh": .number(3)])
        let report = try await h.run([remap])
        #expect(report.error(remap) == "Remap's from range is empty (both ends are 3).")
    }

    @Test func anEmptyToRangeIsFineAndAFromRangeBackwardsIsFine() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let x = xField(&h)
        let flat = h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(0), "fromHigh": .number(20), "toLow": .number(2), "toHigh": .number(2),
        ])
        let backwards = h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(20), "fromHigh": .number(0), "toLow": .number(0), "toHigh": .number(1),
        ])

        h.wire(x, "field", to: backwards, "field")

        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(flat), at: line) == [2, 2, 2, 2, 2])
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(backwards), at: line) == [1, 1, 0.5, 0, 0])
    }

    @Test func remapRefusesRangeEndsThatAreNotFiniteNumbers() async throws {
        // A wired infinity can't be typed (the editor refuses it), so the node is called directly.
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(FieldRemapNode.typeID)
        let inputs = NodeInputs(item: 0, slots: [
            "field": .item(.field(.constant(0))), "fromLow": .item(.number(0)), "fromHigh": .item(.number(.infinity)),
            "toLow": .item(.number(0)), "toHigh": .item(.number(1)), "clamp": .item(.bool(true)),

        }
    }

    // MARK: Clamp

    @Test func clampHoldsAFieldBetweenItsLimits() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let x = xField(&h)
        let clamp = h.add(FieldClampNode.self, ["low": .number(0), "high": .number(15)])
        h.wire(x, "field", to: clamp, "field")
        let report = try await h.run([clamp])
        #expect(report.field(clamp)?.description == "clamp(linear(length 1), 0…15)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(clamp), at: line) == [0, 0, 10, 15, 15])
    }

    @Test func clampWithTheLimitsTheWrongWayRoundFailsAndEqualLimitsDoNot() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let wrong = h.add(FieldClampNode.self, ["low": .number(3), "high": .number(1)])
        let equal = h.add(FieldClampNode.self, ["low": .number(2), "high": .number(2)])
        let report = try await h.run([wrong, equal])
        #expect(report.error(wrong) == "Clamp's low end (3) is above its high end (1).")

    // MARK: Smooth Blend

    @Test func smoothBlendDipsBelowTheMinimumWithinItsWidthAndMatchesItBeyond() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let blend = h.add(FieldSmoothBlendNode.self, ["a": .number(0), "b": .number(1), "width": .number(2)])
        let far = h.add(FieldSmoothBlendNode.self, ["a": .number(0), "b": .number(5), "width": .number(2)])
        let plain = h.add(FieldSmoothBlendNode.self, ["a": .number(3), "b": .number(1), "width": .number(0)])
        let report = try await h.run([blend, far, plain])
        #expect(report.field(blend)?.description == "smoothMin(0, 1, width 2)")
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(blend), at: [.zero]) == [-0.125])
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(far), at: [.zero]) == [0])
        #expect(try await sampled(report.field(plain), at: [.zero]) == [1], "width 0 is the plain minimum")

    @Test func aNegativeWidthIsRefused() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let blend = h.add(FieldSmoothBlendNode.self, ["width": .number(-1)])
        let report = try await h.run([blend])
        #expect(report.error(blend) == "“width” must be 0 or more.")
    }

    // MARK: types (D4)

    @Test func aFieldCannotBeWiredIntoANumberSocketButANumberCanBeWiredIntoAFieldOne() {
        var h = Harness()
        let radial = h.add(RadialGradientNode.self)
        let circle = h.add(CircleNode.self)
        let number = h.add(NumberNode.self)
        let add = h.add(FieldAddNode.self)
        let registry = BuiltInNodes.registry
        let toNumber = h.graph.connectionProblem(from: Endpoint(node: radial.id, socket: "field"),
                                                 to: Endpoint(node: circle.id, socket: "diameter"), registry: registry)
        #expect(toNumber == .typeMismatch(from: .field, to: .number))
        let toField = h.graph.connectionProblem(from: Endpoint(node: number.id, socket: "value"),
                                                to: Endpoint(node: add.id, socket: "a"), registry: registry)
        #expect(toField == nil)
        let fieldToField = h.graph.connectionProblem(from: Endpoint(node: radial.id, socket: "field"),
                                                     to: Endpoint(node: add.id, socket: "b"), registry: registry)
        #expect(fieldToField == nil)
    }

    @Test func aCombinedFieldKeepsTheSourcesItWasBuiltFrom() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let noise = h.add(NoiseFieldNode.self, ["seed": .integer(4)])
        let remap = h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(0), "fromHigh": .number(1), "toLow": .number(10), "toHigh": .number(20),
        ])
        let multiply = h.add(FieldMultiplyNode.self, ["b": .number(2)])
        h.wire(noise, "field", to: remap, "field")
        h.wire(remap, "field", to: multiply, "a")
        let report = try await h.run([multiply])
        #expect(report.field(multiply)?.description == "remap(noise(seed 4, scale 10, octaves 1), 0…1 → 10…20) × 2")
        let values = try await sampled(report.field(multiply), at: (0..<50).map { Vector3(Double($0) * 0.9, 1, 2) })
        #expect(values.allSatisfy { (20.0...40.0).contains($0) })
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Maths/FieldAddNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// a + b, where each is a field or a number (fields spec §3.2). A number typed or wired into either side is a constant field.
public enum FieldAddNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.fieldAdd"
    public static let displayName = "Add"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("a", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("b", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Add", controls: [.number("a"), .number("b")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.add(try inputs.field("a"), try inputs.field("b")))])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Maths/FieldMultiplyNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// a × b (fields spec §3.2). "Multiply by 0.5" is 0.5 typed into `b`.
public enum FieldMultiplyNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.fieldMultiply"
    public static let displayName = "Multiply"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("a", .field, defaultValue: .number(1)),
        SocketSpec("b", .field, defaultValue: .number(1)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Multiply", controls: [.number("a"), .number("b")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.multiply(try inputs.field("a"), try inputs.field("b")))])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Maths/FieldMinMaxNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// The lower or the higher of two fields at each point (fields spec §3.2).
public enum FieldMinMaxNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.fieldMinMax"
    public static let displayName = "Min / Max"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let modes = ["Min", "Max"]
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("a", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("b", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("mode", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(0)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Fields", controls: [.number("a"), .number("b")]),
        InspectorSection(title: "Pick", controls: [.segmented("mode", options: modes)]),
    ]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let (a, b) = (try inputs.field("a"), try inputs.field("b"))
        let picksMax = try inputs.choice("mode", options: modes) == 1
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(picksMax ? .maximum(a, b) : .minimum(a, b))])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Maths/FieldRemapNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// Maps a field's range onto another (fields spec §3.2): `fromLow…fromHigh` onto `toLow…toHigh`, linearly, and held to the
/// output range while `clamp` is on. The ends may run either way (`0…20 → 1…0` fades out). Equal from ends can't be mapped
/// and fail with a message.
public enum FieldRemapNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.fieldRemap"
    public static let displayName = "Remap"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("field", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("fromLow", .number, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("fromHigh", .number, defaultValue: .number(1)),
        SocketSpec("toLow", .number, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("toHigh", .number, defaultValue: .number(1)),
        SocketSpec("clamp", .bool, defaultValue: .bool(true)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "From", controls: [.number("fromLow"), .number("fromHigh")]),
        InspectorSection(title: "To", controls: [.number("toLow"), .number("toHigh")]),
        InspectorSection(title: "Limits", controls: [.toggle("clamp", label: "Clamp to the output range")]),
    ]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let field = try inputs.field("field")
        let (fromLow, fromHigh) = (try inputs.number("fromLow"), try inputs.number("fromHigh"))
        let (toLow, toHigh) = (try inputs.number("toLow"), try inputs.number("toHigh"))
        guard [fromLow, fromHigh, toLow, toHigh].allSatisfy(\.isFinite) else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Remap's ranges must be finite numbers.")
        }
        guard fromLow != fromHigh else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Remap's from range is empty (both ends are \(fromLow.display)).")
        }
        let mapped = Field.remap(field, fromLow: fromLow, fromHigh: fromHigh, toLow: toLow, toHigh: toHigh,
                                 clamped: try inputs.bool("clamp"))
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(mapped)])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Maths/FieldClampNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// Holds a field between two numbers (fields spec §3.2). A `low` above `high` fails; equal ends make the field constant.
public enum FieldClampNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.fieldClamp"
    public static let displayName = "Clamp"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("field", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("low", .number, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("high", .number, defaultValue: .number(1)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [InspectorSection(title: "Limits", controls: [.number("low"), .number("high")])]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let field = try inputs.field("field")
        let (low, high) = (try inputs.number("low"), try inputs.number("high"))
        guard low.isFinite, high.isFinite else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("Clamp's limits must be finite numbers.") }
        guard low <= high else {
            throw NodeError.invalidValue("Clamp's low end (\(low.display)) is above its high end (\(high.display)).")
        }
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.clamp(field, low: low, high: high))])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Maths/FieldSmoothBlendNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// The polynomial smooth minimum of two fields over `width` (fields spec §3.2): where they are further apart than the width
/// it is the plain minimum, and nearer it dips below by up to a quarter of the width, so two shapes' distances merge with a
/// soft join. Width 0 is the plain minimum.
public enum FieldSmoothBlendNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.fieldSmoothBlend"
    public static let displayName = "Smooth Blend"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("a", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("b", .field, defaultValue: .number(0)),
        SocketSpec("width", .number, defaultValue: .number(1)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("field", .field)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Fields", controls: [.number("a"), .number("b")]),
        InspectorSection(title: "Blend", controls: [.number("width")]),
    ]

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let (a, b) = (try inputs.field("a"), try inputs.field("b"))
        let width = try inputs.number("width")
        guard width.isFinite, width >= 0 else { throw NodeError.invalidValue("“width” must be 0 or more.") }
        return NodeOutputs(["field": .field(.smoothMinimum(a, b, width: width))])
    }
}
```

### Task 9: Sample Field and Cull by Field

**Files:**
- Create: `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Using/SampleFieldNode.swift`, `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Using/CullByFieldNode.swift`
- Modify: `Sources/CreatorNodes/BuiltInNodes.swift`
- Modify: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/OutputNodeTests.swift` (62 → 64 nodes, `.fields: 12`), `Tests/CreatorEditorTests/FieldsLibraryTests.swift` (the names, and the palette search)
- Test: `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/SampleFieldNodeTests.swift`, `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/CullByFieldNodeTests.swift`, `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/CulledByFieldPickTests.swift`, `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/GradedHolesTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FieldSampler.sample(_:at:kernel:cache:)`, `EvalContext.fieldDistances` (Tasks 4, 6), `DataTree.mappingItems`/`keepingItems`/`leaves`,
  `SeededRandom.Purpose.fieldCull` (Task 5), the Task 7 test helpers.
- Produces: `SampleFieldNode` (`creator.sampleField`; inputs `field`, `at` (a `plane` socket, `tree` access); output `values`,
  numbers) and `CullByFieldNode` (`creator.cullByField`; inputs `placements` (`tree`), `field`, `mode`, `level`, `keep`, `seed`;
  output `placements`).

**Sample Field** (fields spec §3.3) returns numbers in exactly the shape of `at`: the same nesting, the same paths, culled
gaps where `at` had them, each number the field at that placement's origin. One `FieldSampler` call covers the whole tree.
A vector wired into `at` is a plane through it, so points and placements both wire. A single plane gives a one-number
list (a socket with `tree` access receives a single item as a list of one, `BroadcastPlan`, so the node cannot tell the two
apart; downstream, a list of one broadcasts like one number). This is the graded-sizes path, and its order is **a field →
Remap → Sample Field → a shortcut's size socket**, paired per instance by `InstanceGrid`: Remap takes and returns a *field* and
there is no numeric Remap, so the spec's "Sample Field → Remap → size" (§3.3, §8) cannot be wired (a Remap output into a number
socket is a type mismatch, field → number); Task 12 records an erratum. `gradedHolesFollowTheFieldAcrossThePlate` builds the
chain on OCCT.

**Cull by Field** takes a tree of placements and a field and keeps the placements where the field says, returning the same
nesting with a culled list where items went (7a spec §4, so instance picks survive as with the other cull nodes).
- *Threshold*: Above keeps a placement whose value is at least `level`, Below one whose value is at most `level` (a value exactly on
  the level is kept either way, as Cull by Region keeps a placement exactly at its distance).
- *Probability*: keeps a placement when a `SeededRandom` draw is below the field's value held to 0…1, so 0 keeps nothing and 1
  everything. The draw is `unit(at: the item's kept index)` of the stream `branch ordinal`, purpose `.fieldCull`, exactly
  how Random Reduce draws (7a spec §4): it depends on the seed and the placement's place, never on how many there are, and a
  cull before this node changes no survivor's draw.
Both refuse a list wired into any of their inputs, in the layouts' words (`singleValuesRefusal` = `LayoutChecks.singleValues`:
"Sample Field takes single values, not lists: wire one value into each input, or pick one out of the list first with List
Item."), because they work on a whole tree at once and a `tree`-access socket cannot also broadcast. The spec (§2.1) would allow
a field per branch; decision 11 refuses it. The sentence names no socket because the refusal runs before the node and cannot tell
which socket held the list.

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Sample Field (fields spec §3.3): numbers in exactly the shape of `at`, paths and culled gaps included.
struct SampleFieldNodeTests {
    /// Four placements along +X, 10 mm apart from the origin, then (in this order) a Partition into rows of `rows` and a Cull
    /// Pattern `mask`, into the `at` of a Sample Field whose field is x ÷ 10 (so the placements sample 0, 1, 2, 3), unless
    /// `xField` is false and the test wires its own.
    struct Rig {
        var h = Harness()
        let placements: Node, sample: Node

        init(rows: Int? = nil, mask: String? = nil, xField: Bool = true) {
            let grid = h.add(GridPointsNode.self, [
                "countX": .integer(4), "countY": .integer(1), "spacingX": .number(10), "centred": .bool(false),
            ])
            placements = h.add(PointsToPlacementsNode.self)
            sample = h.add(SampleFieldNode.self)
            h.wire(grid, "points", to: placements, "points")
            var (source, socket): (Node, SocketName) = (placements, "placements")
            if let rows {
                let partition = h.add(PartitionNode.self, ["size": .integer(rows)])
                h.wire(source, socket, to: partition, "tree")
                (source, socket) = (partition, "tree")
            }
            if let mask {
                let before = h.add(CullPatternNode.self, [NodeSetting.cullPattern: .text(mask)])
                h.wire(source, socket, to: before, "tree")
                (source, socket) = (before, "tree")
            }
            h.wire(source, socket, to: sample, "at")
            if xField {
                let plane = h.add(PlaneNode.self, ["orientation": .integer(2)])  // YZ: its normal is +X
                let field = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(10)])
                h.wire(plane, "plane", to: field, "plane")
                h.wire(field, "field", to: sample, "field")
            }
        }
    }

    func numbers(_ report: EvaluationReport, _ node: Node) -> [[Double]] {
        (report.value(node, "values")?.asTree?.leaves ?? []).map { leaf in
            leaf.items.compactMap { if case .number(let value) = $0 { value } else { nil } }
        }
    }

    // MARK: shape

    @Test func aFlatListGivesAFlatListOfNumbers() async throws {
        let rig = Rig()
        let report = try await rig.h.run([rig.sample])
        #expect(report.value(rig.sample, "values")?.numbers == [0, 1, 2, 3])
        #expect(report.value(rig.sample, "values")?.isList == true)
        #expect(report.isOK(rig.sample))
    }

    @Test func aTreeKeepsItsBranchesAndPaths() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(rows: 2)
        let report = try await rig.h.run([rig.sample])
        #expect(numbers(report, rig.sample) == [[0, 1], [2, 3]])
        #expect(report.positions(rig.sample, "values") == [[0, 1], [0, 1]])
        #expect(report.tree(rig.sample, "values")?.depth == 2)
    }

    @Test func culledGapsStayAndTheNumbersBelongToTheSurvivors() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(mask: "1 0")  // keeps {0} and {2}
        let report = try await rig.h.run([rig.sample])
        #expect(report.positions(rig.sample, "values") == [[0, 2]], "the numbers keep the places of the placements")
        #expect(numbers(report, rig.sample) == [[0, 2]], "the field at x = 0 and x = 20, not at the culled ones")
        #expect(report.tree(rig.sample, "values")?.isCulled == true)
    }

    @Test func aSinglePlaneGivesAListOfOneNumber() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let plane = h.add(PlaneNode.self, ["offset": .number(5)])
        let gradient = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self)  // z ÷ 10
        let sample = h.add(SampleFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(plane, "plane", to: sample, "at")
        h.wire(gradient, "field", to: sample, "field")
        let report = try await h.run([sample])
        #expect(report.value(sample, "values")?.numbers == [0.5])
    }

    @Test func aVectorWiredIntoAtIsAPlaneThroughIt() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let point = h.add(VectorNode.self, ["x": .number(0), "y": .number(0), "z": .number(20)])
        let gradient = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self)
        let sample = h.add(SampleFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(point, "vector", to: sample, "at")
        h.wire(gradient, "field", to: sample, "field")
        #expect(try await h.run([sample]).value(sample, "values")?.numbers == [2])
    }

    @Test func aNumberTypedIntoFieldSamplesAsThatConstant() async throws {
        var rig = Rig(xField: false)
        let typed = rig.h.add(SampleFieldNode.self, ["field": .number(7)])
        rig.h.wire(rig.placements, "placements", to: typed, "at")
        let report = try await rig.h.run([typed])
        #expect(report.value(typed, "values")?.numbers == [7, 7, 7, 7])
    }

    // MARK: kernel use and the cache

    @Test func aSolidDistanceIsOneKernelCallForTheWholeTreeAndIsRememberedAcrossEdits() async throws {
        var rig = Rig(xField: false)
        let box = rig.h.box(10, 10, 10)
        let distance = rig.h.add(DistanceFieldNode.self)
        let remap = rig.h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(-5), "fromHigh": .number(30), "toLow": .number(0), "toHigh": .number(1),
        ])
        rig.h.wire(box, "solid", to: distance, "target")
        rig.h.wire(distance, "field", to: remap, "field")
        rig.h.wire(remap, "field", to: rig.sample, "field")
        let kernel = FakeKernel()
        let evaluator = Evaluator(registry: BuiltInNodes.registry, kernel: kernel)
        let demand: Set<NodeID> = [rig.sample.id]
        let first = try await evaluator.evaluate(rig.h.graph, demand: demand)
        #expect(first.isOK(rig.sample))
        #expect(await kernel.operationLog.filter { $0 == "distances" }.count == 1, "one call for the four placements")
        // Edit the remap: a new field over the same distance source and points, so the kernel is not asked again.
        rig.h.set(remap, "toHigh", .number(2))
        let second = try await evaluator.evaluate(rig.h.graph, demand: demand)
        #expect(second.isOK(rig.sample))
        #expect(await kernel.operationLog.filter { $0 == "distances" }.count == 1, "the cache answered")
        let before = try #require(first.value(rig.sample, "values")?.numbers)
        let after = try #require(second.value(rig.sample, "values")?.numbers)
        #expect(zip(before, after).allSatisfy { abs($0 * 2 - $1) < 1e-12 }, "toHigh 1 → 2 doubled every number")
    }

    @Test func aKernelLimitIsTheSamplingNodesError() async throws {
        let solid = try await FakeKernel().extrude(.rectangle(width: 10, height: 10, plane: .xy), distance: 10, mode: .oneSided,
                                                   tag: NodeTag(node: NodeID(), item: 0))
        let curved = FaceInfo(id: FaceID(0), kind: .cylinder, normal: .unitZ, area: 1, centroid: .zero, tags: [])
        let round = Solid(topology: Topology(faces: [curved], edges: []), bounds: solid.bounds, storage: BareStorage())
        let node = BuiltInNodes.registry.makeNode(SampleFieldNode.typeID)
        let field = Field.distance(.kernel(.faces(FaceSet(solid: round, faces: [FaceID(0)]))), signed: false)
        let inputs = NodeInputs(item: 0, slots: ["field": .item(.field(field)), "at": .tree(.list([.plane(.xy)]))])
        do {
            _ = try await SampleFieldNode.evaluate(inputs, kernel: FakeKernel(), context: EvalContext(node: node, item: 0, parameters: [:]))
            Issue.record("expected the kernel's refusal")
        } catch {
            #expect(Evaluator.message(for: error) == "Distance to a curved face isn't supported by this kernel yet.")
        }
    }

    // MARK: refusals

    @Test func withoutAFieldTheNodeWaits() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let plane = h.add(PlaneNode.self)
        let sample = h.add(SampleFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(plane, "plane", to: sample, "at")
        let report = try await h.run([sample])
        #expect(report.state(sample) == .idle("Connect or set “field”."))
    }

    @Test func aListOfFieldsIsRefusedInWords() async throws {
        var rig = Rig(xField: false)
        let series = rig.h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["count": .integer(3)])
        rig.h.wire(series, "values", to: rig.sample, "field")
        let report = try await rig.h.run([rig.sample])
        #expect(report.error(rig.sample) == "Sample Field takes single values, not lists: wire one value into each input, "
                + "or pick one out of the list first with List Item.")
    }

    @Test func aTreeOfNothingGivesNothing() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(mask: "0")
        let report = try await rig.h.run([rig.sample])
        #expect(report.isOK(rig.sample))
        #expect(report.tree(rig.sample, "values")?.itemCount == 0)
    }
}

/// Storage for a hand-built solid.
private final class BareStorage: SolidStorage {
    var estimatedBytes: Int { 0 }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/CullByFieldNodeTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import Testing
@testable import CreatorNodes

/// Cull by Field (fields spec §3.3): Threshold and Probability, over a tree, keeping paths.
struct CullByFieldNodeTests {
    /// `count` placements along +X from the origin, 10 mm apart, then (in this order) a Partition into rows of `rows`, a Cull
    /// Pattern `mask`, and the Cull by Field with `settings`. Its field is x ÷ 10 unless `xField` is false, when `settings`
    /// should hold a number for "field".
    struct Rig {
        var h = Harness()
        let cull: Node

        init(count: Int = 4, rows: Int? = nil, mask: String? = nil, xField: Bool = true, _ settings: [SocketName: ConstantValue] = [:]) {
            let grid = h.add(GridPointsNode.self, [
                "countX": .integer(count), "countY": .integer(1), "spacingX": .number(10), "centred": .bool(false),
            ])
            let placements = h.add(PointsToPlacementsNode.self)
            cull = h.add(CullByFieldNode.self, settings)
            h.wire(grid, "points", to: placements, "points")
            var (source, socket): (Node, SocketName) = (placements, "placements")
            if let rows {
                let partition = h.add(PartitionNode.self, ["size": .integer(rows)])
                h.wire(source, socket, to: partition, "tree")
                (source, socket) = (partition, "tree")
            }
            if let mask {
                let before = h.add(CullPatternNode.self, [NodeSetting.cullPattern: .text(mask)])
                h.wire(source, socket, to: before, "tree")
                (source, socket) = (before, "tree")
            }
            h.wire(source, socket, to: cull, "placements")
            if xField {
                let plane = h.add(PlaneNode.self, ["orientation": .integer(2)])  // YZ: its normal is +X
                let field = h.add(LinearGradientNode.self, ["length": .number(10)])
                h.wire(plane, "plane", to: field, "plane")
                h.wire(field, "field", to: cull, "field")
            }
        }

        func run() async throws -> EvaluationReport { try await h.run([cull]) }

        func kept() async throws -> [[Int]]? { try await run().positions(cull, "placements") }
    }

    /// Probability over `count` placements, a constant field `value`, seed `seed`.
    func probability(_ value: Double, seed: Int, count: Int = 10, rows: Int? = nil, mask: String? = nil) -> Rig {
        Rig(count: count, rows: rows, mask: mask, xField: false, ["mode": .integer(1), "seed": .integer(seed), "field": .number(value)])
    }

    // MARK: Threshold

    @Test func aboveKeepsWhatIsAtLeastTheLevel() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(count: 4, ["level": .number(1.5), "keep": .integer(0)])
        #expect(try await rig.kept() == [[2, 3]])
    }

    @Test func belowKeepsWhatIsAtMostTheLevel() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(count: 4, ["level": .number(1.5), "keep": .integer(1)])
        #expect(try await rig.kept() == [[0, 1]])
    }

    @Test func aValueExactlyOnTheLevelIsKeptEitherWay() async throws {
        let above = Rig(count: 4, ["level": .number(2), "keep": .integer(0)])
        let below = Rig(count: 4, ["level": .number(2), "keep": .integer(1)])
        #expect(try await above.kept() == [[2, 3]])
        #expect(try await below.kept() == [[0, 1, 2]])
    }

    @Test func theDefaultKeepsWhatIsAboveHalf() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(count: 4)
        #expect(try await rig.kept() == [[1, 2, 3]], "values 0, 1, 2, 3 against 0.5")
    }

    // MARK: Probability

    @Test func aFieldOfZeroKeepsNothingOneKeepsEverythingAndOutOfRangeIsHeld() async throws {
        for (value, count) in [(0.0, 0), (1.0, 10), (-3.0, 0), (7.0, 10)] {
            let rig = probability(value, seed: 5)
            let report = try await rig.run()
            #expect(report.tree(rig.cull, "placements")?.itemCount == count, "a field of \(value)")
            #expect(report.isOK(rig.cull), "a seed never warns")
        }
    }

    @Test func probabilityIsPinnedToTheSeededDraws() async throws {
        let rig = probability(0.5, seed: 1)
        let kept = try #require(try await rig.kept()?.first)
        let random = SeededRandom(seed: 1, stream: 0, purpose: .fieldCull)
        #expect(kept == (0..<10).filter { random.unit(at: $0) < 0.5 }, "draw k of the stream decides place k")
        // Seed 1's first ten draws are 0.74 0.84 0.33 0.90 0.34 0.47 0.92 0.07 0.62 0.30, worked out from the generator.
        #expect(kept == [2, 4, 5, 7, 9], "the draws are pinned: \(kept)")
    }

    @Test func theSameSeedGivesTheSameCullAndAnotherSeedAnother() async throws {
        #expect(try await probability(0.5, seed: 3, count: 40).kept() == probability(0.5, seed: 3, count: 40).kept())
        #expect(try await probability(0.5, seed: 3, count: 40).kept() != probability(0.5, seed: 4, count: 40).kept())
    }

    @Test func moreItemsAtTheEndChangeNoEarlierDecision() async throws {
        let short = try #require(try await probability(0.5, seed: 9, count: 12).kept()?.first)
        let long = try #require(try await probability(0.5, seed: 9, count: 20).kept()?.first)
        #expect(long.filter { $0 < 12 } == short)
    }

    @Test func eachBranchDrawsFromItsOwnStream() async throws {
        let rig = probability(0.5, seed: 7, count: 12, rows: 4)
        let kept = try #require(try await rig.kept())
        #expect(kept.count == 3)
        #expect(Set(kept).count > 1, "three branches do not repeat each other's draws: \(kept)")
        for (ordinal, positions) in kept.enumerated() {
            let random = SeededRandom(seed: 7, stream: ordinal, purpose: .fieldCull)
            #expect(positions == (0..<4).filter { random.unit(at: $0) < 0.5 })
        }
    }

    @Test func aCullBeforeChangesNoSurvivorsDraw() async throws {
        let everything = try #require(try await probability(0.5, seed: 42).kept()?.first)
        let survivors = try #require(try await probability(0.5, seed: 42, mask: "1 0").kept()?.first)  // "1 0" keeps the even places
        #expect(survivors == everything.filter { $0.isMultiple(of: 2) }, "an item decides as it would have, at its own index")
    }

    // MARK: shape and paths

    @Test func aTreeKeepsItsNestingWithCulledListsWhereItemsWent() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(count: 6, rows: 3, ["level": .number(2.5), "keep": .integer(0)])
        let report = try await rig.run()
        #expect(report.positions(rig.cull, "placements") == [[], [0, 1, 2]], "values 0 1 2 | 3 4 5")
        #expect(report.tree(rig.cull, "placements")?.depth == 2)
        #expect(report.tree(rig.cull, "placements")?.isCulled == true)
    }

    @Test func survivorsKeepTheIndicesTheyHad() async throws {
        let lower = Rig(count: 6, ["level": .number(2.5), "keep": .integer(1)])
        #expect(try await lower.kept() == [[0, 1, 2]])
        let upper = Rig(count: 6, ["level": .number(2.5), "keep": .integer(0)])
        let rest = try #require(try await upper.run().tree(upper.cull, "placements"))
        #expect(rest.leaves.first?.positions == [3, 4, 5], "{3}, {4} and {5}, not renumbered from 0")
        #expect(rest.isCulled)
    }

    @Test func aCulledListFromBeforeIsJudgedBySurvivorsAtTheirPlaces() async throws {
        let rig = Rig(count: 6, mask: "0 1", ["level": .number(2.5), "keep": .integer(0)])  // "0 1" keeps the places 1, 3 and 5
        #expect(try await rig.kept() == [[3, 5]], "values 1, 3 and 5 against 2.5")
    }

    // MARK: refusals

    @Test func aBadModeASideAndAMissingInputAreSaidPlainly() async throws {
        let bad = Rig(count: 3, ["mode": .integer(3)])
        #expect(try await bad.run().error(bad.cull) == "Choose Threshold or Probability for “mode”.")
        let badSide = Rig(count: 3, ["keep": .integer(2)])
        #expect(try await badSide.run().error(badSide.cull) == "Choose Above or Below for “keep”.")
        var open = Harness()
        let cull = open.add(CullByFieldNode.self)
        #expect(try await open.run([cull]).state(cull) == .idle("Connect or set “placements”."))
    }

    @Test func aListOfFieldsIsRefusedInWords() async throws {
        var h = Harness()
        let grid = h.add(GridPointsNode.self)
        let placements = h.add(PointsToPlacementsNode.self)
        let series = h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["count": .integer(3)])
        let cull = h.add(CullByFieldNode.self)
        h.wire(grid, "points", to: placements, "points")
        h.wire(placements, "placements", to: cull, "placements")
        h.wire(series, "values", to: cull, "field")
        #expect(try await h.run([cull]).error(cull) == Self.listRefusal)
    }

    /// The refusal runs before the node, so it names no socket: a list on `level` gets the same sentence as a list of fields.
    @Test func aListInAnotherInputIsRefusedInTheSameWords() async throws {
        var rig = Rig(count: 4)
        let series = rig.h.add(SeriesNode.self, ["count": .integer(3)])
        rig.h.wire(series, "values", to: rig.cull, "level")
        #expect(try await rig.run().error(rig.cull) == Self.listRefusal)
    }

    static let listRefusal = "Cull by Field takes single values, not lists: wire one value into each input, "
        + "or pick one out of the list first with List Item."
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/CulledByFieldPickTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
@testable import CreatorNodes
import CreatorOCCT
import Testing

/// Patterns spec §6 and §9 through Cull by Field: a pick on one instance survives a field cull of another, and a pick on a
/// culled instance warns, naming its path, and selects nothing (the existing rule, as for Cull Index). A 3 × 2 plate of Ø5
/// holes 15 mm apart: hole {1} is at (0, −7.5). The field is the distance from a centre ÷ 15, culling what is below a
/// half, so a centre on a hole culls that hole alone and a centre far away culls nothing.
struct CulledByFieldPickTests {
    struct Fixture {
        var plate = PatternPlate(columns: 3, rows: 2, spacingX: 15, spacingY: 15)
        let centre: Node, holes: Node, rule: Node, fillet: Node

        init() {
            centre = plate.h.add(VectorNode.self, ["x": .number(1000), "y": .number(1000)])
            let field = plate.h.add(RadialGradientNode.self, ["radius": .number(15)])
            let cull = plate.h.add(CullByFieldNode.self, ["level": .number(0.5), "keep": .integer(0)])
            holes = plate.h.add(HolePatternNode.self, ["diameter": .number(5), "depth": .number(6)])
            rule = plate.h.add(EdgesByTagNode.self)
            fillet = plate.h.add(FilletNode.self, ["radius": .number(0.5)])
            plate.h.wire(centre, "vector", to: field, "center")
            plate.h.wire(field, "field", to: cull, "field")
            plate.h.wire(plate.placements, "placements", to: cull, "placements")
            plate.h.wire(plate.plate, "solid", to: holes, "part")
            plate.h.wire(cull, "placements", to: holes, "placements")
            plate.h.wire(holes, "solid", to: rule, "solid")
            plate.h.wire(rule, "edges", to: fillet, "edges")
        }

        /// Culls hole {1} (the field is 0 there) and no other.
        mutating func cullHoleOne() {
            plate.h.set(centre, "x", .number(0))
            plate.h.set(centre, "y", .number(-7.5))
        }

        mutating func cullNothing() {
            plate.h.set(centre, "x", .number(1000))
            plate.h.set(centre, "y", .number(1000))
        }

        func bore(_ index: Int) -> TopoTag {
            TopoTag(node: NodeID.instanceScoped([holes.id, holes.id]), item: index, role: .side(segment: 1))
        }

        /// The rim of hole `index` on the plate's top.
        func rim(of index: Int, in solid: Solid) -> EdgeInfo? {
            let top = TopoTag(node: plate.plate.id, item: 0, role: .endCap)
            return solid.topology.edges.first { edge in
                guard edge.kind == .circle, !edge.isSeam, edge.faces.count == 2 else { return false }
                let sides = edge.faces.compactMap { solid.topology.face($0) }
                return sides.contains { $0.tags.contains(bore(index)) } && sides.contains { $0.tags.contains(top) }
            }
        }

        /// Picks the rims of the holes `indices` on the rule, from a run with nothing culled.
        mutating func pick(_ indices: [Int], kernel: any Kernel) async throws {
            cullNothing()
            let solid = try onlySolid(try await plate.h.run([holes], kernel: kernel), holes)
            let edges = try indices.map { try #require(rim(of: $0, in: solid)).id }
            plate.h.set(rule, NodeSetting.picks, .edgePicks(solid.topology.picks(for: edges)))
        }

        func selected(_ report: EvaluationReport) throws -> (solid: Solid, edges: [EdgeInfo]) {
            let set = try #require(report.value(rule, "edges")?.edgeSets?.first)
            return (set.solid, set.edges.compactMap { set.solid.topology.edge($0) })
        }
    }

    @Test func aPickOnOneInstanceSurvivesAFieldCullOfAnother() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        var f = Fixture()
        try await f.pick([4], kernel: kernel)
        let before = try f.selected(try await f.plate.h.run([f.rule], kernel: kernel))
        f.cullHoleOne()
        let report = try await f.plate.h.run([f.fillet], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(report.isOK(f.rule) && report.isOK(f.fillet), "\(String(describing: report.state(f.rule)))")
        let (solid, edges) = try f.selected(report)
        #expect(edges.count == 1)
        #expect(edges.allSatisfy { edge in
            edge.faces.compactMap { solid.topology.face($0) }.contains { $0.tags.contains(f.bore(4)) }
        }, "still the rim of {4}")
        guard case .circle(let after, _, _, _, _)? = edges.first?.curve,
              case .circle(let was, _, _, _, _)? = before.edges.first?.curve else {
            Issue.record("expected circles")
            return
        }
        #expect(isClose(after, was), "the same physical hole: a cull never moves a pick to a neighbour")
    }

    @Test func aFieldCullLeavesTheSurvivorsAtTheirIndices() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        var f = Fixture()
        f.cullHoleOne()
        let report = try await f.plate.h.run([f.holes], kernel: kernel)
        let solid = try onlySolid(report, f.holes)
        let items = Set(solid.topology.faces.flatMap(\.tags).filter(\.node.isInstanceQualified).map(\.item))
        #expect(items == [0, 2, 3, 4, 5], "the holes are {0} and {2} to {5}, not renumbered")
        #expect(report.isOK(f.holes), "a cull never warns")
    }

    @Test func aPickOnACulledInstanceWarnsNamingItAndSelectsNothing() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        var f = Fixture()
        try await f.pick([1], kernel: kernel)
        f.cullHoleOne()
        let report = try await f.plate.h.run([f.rule], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(report.warning(f.rule) == "Instance {1} no longer exists, so the edge picked on it isn't selected.")
        #expect(try f.selected(report).edges.isEmpty, "never a neighbour's rim")
    }
}
```

Create `Tests/CreatorNodesTests/Fields/GradedHolesTests.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel
import CreatorNodes
import CreatorOCCT
import Testing

/// The graded-sizes path (fields spec §3.3) in the one order that wires: a field → Remap → Sample Field → a shortcut's size
/// socket. Remap takes and returns a field, so a Remap after the sampling would put a field into a number socket.
struct GradedHolesTests {
    /// A Radial Gradient of radius 40 centred on one hole of the plate's 2 × 2 grid (at (±20, ±10)), remapped 0…1 → Ø3…Ø5 and
    /// held there, sampled at the four placements into a Hole Pattern's `diameter`. The holes are 0, 0.5, 1 and 1.118 radii
    /// from the centre: Ø3, Ø4, Ø5 and (clamped) Ø5.
    @Test func gradedHolesFollowTheFieldAcrossThePlate() async throws {
        let kernel = OCCTKernel()
        var plate = PatternPlate()
        let centre = plate.h.add(VectorNode.self, ["x": .number(-20), "y": .number(-10)])
        let radial = plate.h.add(RadialGradientNode.self, ["radius": .number(40)])
        let remap = plate.h.add(FieldRemapNode.self, [
            "fromLow": .number(0), "fromHigh": .number(1), "toLow": .number(3), "toHigh": .number(5),
        ])
        let sample = plate.h.add(SampleFieldNode.self)
        let holes = plate.h.add(HolePatternNode.self, ["depth": .number(6)])
        plate.h.wire(centre, "vector", to: radial, "center")
        plate.h.wire(radial, "field", to: remap, "field")
        plate.h.wire(remap, "field", to: sample, "field")
        plate.h.wire(plate.placements, "placements", to: sample, "at")
        plate.h.wire(sample, "values", to: holes, "diameter")
        plate.h.wire(plate.plate, "solid", to: holes, "part")
        plate.h.wire(plate.placements, "placements", to: holes, "placements")
        let report = try await plate.h.run([holes], kernel: kernel)
        #expect(report.isOK(sample) && report.isOK(holes), "\(String(describing: report.state(holes)))")

        // The numbers are the field at each placement, in the placements' order.
        let origins = (report.value(plate.placements, "placements")?.items ?? []).compactMap { item -> Vector3? in
            if case .plane(let plane) = item { plane.origin } else { nil }
        }
        let sizes = try #require(report.value(sample, "values")?.numbers)
        #expect(origins.count == 4 && sizes.count == 4)
        let expected = origins.map { min(5, 3 + 2 * ($0 - Vector3(-20, -10, 0)).length / 40) }
        #expect(zip(sizes, expected).allSatisfy { abs($0 - $1) < 1e-9 }, "got \(sizes), wanted \(expected)")
        #expect(sizes.sorted().map { ($0 * 1000).rounded() / 1000 } == [3, 4, 5, 5])

        // And the holes cut are those sizes, paired with the placements by InstanceGrid: the plate loses Ø3 + Ø4 + Ø5 + Ø5.
        let want = [3.0, 4, 5, 5].reduce(0) { $0 + PatternPlate.cylinderVolume($1, 6) }
        let removed = PatternPlate.plateVolume - (try await volume(try onlySolid(report, holes), kernel))
        #expect(isClose(removed, want), "removed \(removed), wanted \(want)")
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Using/SampleFieldNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// Turns a field into numbers (fields spec §3.3): the field at each placement's origin, in exactly the shape of `at`, paths and
/// culled gaps included. One `FieldSampler` call covers the whole tree (kernel distances go through the evaluator's cache). A
/// vector wired into `at` is a plane through it, so points and placements both wire. This is the graded-sizes path, in the one
/// order that wires: a field → Remap → Sample Field → a shortcut's size socket, paired per instance by `InstanceGrid` (Remap takes
/// and returns a field, so it comes before the sampling, never after).
public enum SampleFieldNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.sampleField"
    public static let displayName = "Sample Field"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("field", .field),
        SocketSpec("at", .plane, access: .tree),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("values", .number)]
    public static var singleValuesRefusal: String? { LayoutChecks.singleValues(displayName) }

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let tree = try inputs.tree("at")
        let origins = try tree.items.map { scalar -> Vector3 in
            guard case .plane(let plane) = scalar else { throw NodeError.typeMismatch("at", expected: .plane) }
            return plane.origin
        }
        let values = try await FieldSampler.sample(try inputs.field("field"), at: origins, kernel: kernel, cache: context.fieldDistances)
        var next = 0
        let numbers = tree.mappingItems { _, _, _ in
            defer { next += 1 }
            return .number(values[next])
        }
        return NodeOutputs(trees: ["values": numbers])
    }
}
```

Create `Sources/CreatorNodes/Fields/Using/CullByFieldNode.swift`:

```swift
import CreatorGeometry
import CreatorGraph
import CreatorKernel

/// Keeps placements where a field says (fields spec §3.3), returning the same nesting with a culled list where items went, so
/// instance picks survive as with the other cull nodes (patterns spec §4 and §6).
/// - Threshold: Above keeps a placement whose field is at least `level`, Below one whose field is at most `level`.
/// - Probability: keeps a placement when a `SeededRandom` draw is below the field held to 0…1. The draw is the item's kept
///   index in the stream of its branch's ordinal, purpose `.fieldCull`, exactly how Random Reduce draws, so it depends on the
///   seed and the place alone and a cull before this node changes no survivor's draw.
public enum CullByFieldNode: NodeDefinition {
    public static let typeID = "creator.cullByField"
    public static let displayName = "Cull by Field"
    public static let category = NodeCategory.fields
    public static let modes = ["Threshold", "Probability"]
    public static let sides = ["Above", "Below"]
    public static let inputs = [
        SocketSpec("placements", .plane, access: .tree),
        SocketSpec("field", .field),
        SocketSpec("mode", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(0)),
        SocketSpec("level", .number, defaultValue: .number(0.5)),
        SocketSpec("keep", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(0)),
        SocketSpec("seed", .integer, defaultValue: .integer(0)),
    ]
    public static let outputs = [SocketSpec("placements", .plane)]
    public static let inspector = [
        InspectorSection(title: "Cull", controls: [.segmented("mode", options: modes)]),
        InspectorSection(title: "Threshold", controls: [.number("level"), .segmented("keep", options: sides)]),
        InspectorSection(title: "Probability", controls: [.integer("seed")]),
    ]
    public static var singleValuesRefusal: String? { LayoutChecks.singleValues(displayName) }

    public static func evaluate(_ inputs: NodeInputs, kernel: any Kernel, context: EvalContext) async throws -> NodeOutputs {
        let tree = try inputs.tree("placements")
        let origins = try tree.items.map { scalar -> Vector3 in
            guard case .plane(let plane) = scalar else { throw NodeError.typeMismatch("placements", expected: .plane) }
            return plane.origin
        }
        let byProbability = try inputs.choice("mode", options: modes) == 1
        let keepsAbove = try inputs.choice("keep", options: sides) == 0
        let (level, seed) = (try inputs.number("level"), try inputs.integer("seed"))
        let values = try await FieldSampler.sample(try inputs.field("field"), at: origins, kernel: kernel, cache: context.fieldDistances)

        // Where each branch's items start in `values`, and each branch's stream of draws.
        let leaves = tree.leaves
        var starts: [Int] = []
        var running = 0
        for leaf in leaves {
            starts.append(running)
            running += leaf.items.count
        }
        let draws = leaves.indices.map { SeededRandom(seed: seed, stream: $0, purpose: .fieldCull) }
        let kept = tree.keepingItems { leaf, ordinal, k in
            let value = values[starts[ordinal] + k]
            if byProbability { return draws[ordinal].unit(at: leaf.positions[k]) < min(max(value, 0), 1) }
            return keepsAbove ? value >= level : value <= level
        }
        return NodeOutputs(trees: ["placements": kept])
    }
}
```
